title: Home

## ising-model

Lean 4 + mathlib formalization of Ising model theorems, with particular
emphasis on Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics: A Functional Integral Point of
View* (2nd ed., 1987).

> **Notice:** automatic publication of the doc-gen4 API reference to
> `/docs/` on GitHub Pages is **currently paused**. Every main-push
> run of the `docs` job in the CI workflow was taking ~1 hour and
> queuing up behind each merge, so the `docs` job in
> `.github/workflows/lean_action_ci.yml` has been commented out. The
> Lean build and tests (`build` job) continue to run on every push
> and pull request. To build the API reference locally, run
> `lake -R -Kenv=dev build IsingModel:docs` and open
> `.lake/build/doc/index.html`.
>

All theorems are formally proved with **zero `sorry`**.
Pre-existing axioms (four items) cover Lebowitz-type inequalities
whose full proofs require measure theory machinery; see the *Axioms*
section below.

## How to read this page

We distinguish three formalization regimes:

1. **Finite-volume** — the Ising model on a fixed finite graph
   `G : SimpleGraph ι` with `[Fintype ι]`.  Most of the project is here.
2. **Discretized infinite-volume** — a fixed finite ambient `ι` with
   growing subgraphs `G₁ ≤ G₂ ≤ ⋯`.  The "Λ ↑" convergence theorems
   of GJ §4.2 and §4.6 are formalized here: the mechanism of proof is
   identical to GJ, but the ambient lattice is finite.
3. **Genuine infinite-volume** — an unbounded ambient type `V : Type*`
   with `Λ : Finset V` finite volumes and an exhaustion `Λₙ ↑ V`.
   Introduced in `IsingModel/AmbientLattice.lean`.

When a GJ theorem is marked "Done", the adjacent *Regime* column
specifies which of the three above apply.

## Formalized theorems

### Correlation inequalities (§4.1–§4.7)

| Theorem | Statement | File | Regime |
|---|---|---|---|
| **GKS-I** (Thm 4.1.1) | `⟨σ^A⟩ ≥ 0` for ferromagnetic `p` | `Inequalities/GKS.lean` | Finite |
| **GKS-II** (Thm 4.1.1) | `⟨σ^A σ^B⟩ ≥ ⟨σ^A⟩⟨σ^B⟩` | `Inequalities/GKS.lean` | Finite |
| **FKG** (§4.4) | `⟨fg⟩ ≥ ⟨f⟩⟨g⟩` for `f, g` monotone | `Inequalities/FKG.lean` | Finite |
| **Boundedness** (Prop 4.2.2) | `|⟨σ^A⟩| ≤ 1` | `InfiniteVolume.lean` / `AmbientLattice.lean` | Finite + genuine ∞-vol |
| **J-monotonicity** (Prop 4.2.1) | `⟨σ^A⟩` monotone in `J ≥ 0` | `InfiniteVolume.lean` / `AmbientLattice.lean` | Finite + genuine ∞-vol |
| **h-monotonicity** (Prop 4.2.4) | `⟨σ^A⟩` monotone in `h ≥ 0` | `InfiniteVolume.lean` / `AmbientLattice.lean` | Finite + genuine ∞-vol |
| **β-monotonicity** | `⟨σ^A⟩` monotone in `β > 0` | `InfiniteVolume.lean` / `AmbientLattice.lean` | Finite + genuine ∞-vol |
| **Subgraph monotonicity** | `G₁ ≤ G₂ ⇒ ⟨σ^A⟩_{G₁} ≤ ⟨σ^A⟩_{G₂}` | `InfiniteVolume.lean` / `AmbientLattice.lean` | Finite + Discretized Λ↑ + genuine ∞-vol |
| **GKS-II at ∞-vol** (Thm 4.2.3) | `⟨σ^A⟩ · ⟨σ^B⟩ ≤ ⟨σ^{A∆B}⟩` | `AmbientLattice.lean` | Genuine ∞-vol (ferromagnetic) |
| **Exhaustion-independence** | `correlationInfinite G Λ = correlationInfinite G Λ'` | `AmbientLattice.lean` | Genuine ∞-vol |
| **Lee–Yang circle theorem** (§4.5) | Ising partition polynomial nonvanishing on polydisk | `LeeYang.lean` | Finite |
| **Lee–Yang (graph form)** | Z ≠ 0 on polydisk for ferromagnetic graph | `FreeEnergy.lean` | Finite |
| **φ⁴ Lebowitz** (Cor 4.3.2) | `lebowitz_third/four/inductive` | `Inequalities/GHS.lean` | Finite, axiom |
| **Cor 4.3.3** | `U₄ ≤ 0` for `h = 0` | `Inequalities/GHS.lean` / `AmbientLattice.lean` | Finite + genuine ∞-vol (`truncated4Infinite_nonpos_h_zero`) |
| **GHS** (Cor 4.3.4) | `⟨σᵢ;σⱼ;σₖ⟩ ≤ 0` | `Inequalities/GHS.lean` / `AmbientLattice.lean` | Finite + genuine ∞-vol (`truncated3Infinite_nonpos`) |
| **Cor 4.3.5** | inductive `n`-point bound (`h = 0`) | `Inequalities/GHS.lean` | Finite |

### §4.2: Thermodynamic limit of correlations (Thm 4.2.3)

Original GJ statement: as `Λ ↑ ℝᵈ`, `⟨σ^B⟩_Λ` converges.
Formalized in three regimes:

| Result | Statement | File | Regime |
|---|---|---|---|
| `correlation_convergent` | `⟨σ^A⟩_{(n,h,β)}` converges as `J = n → ∞` | `InfiniteVolume.lean` | Finite |
| `correlation_convergent_h` | `⟨σ^A⟩_{(J,n,β)}` converges as `h = n → ∞` | `InfiniteVolume.lean` | Finite |
| `correlation_convergent_beta` | `⟨σ^A⟩_{(J,h,n+1)}` converges as `β = n+1 → ∞` | `InfiniteVolume.lean` | Finite |
| `correlation_convergent_subgraph` | `⟨σ^A⟩_{Gₙ}` converges for `Gₙ ↑` | `InfiniteVolume.lean` | Discretized Λ↑ |
| `Ambient.correlationInfinite` | `correlationInfinite := ⨆ n, correlationAlongExhaustion G Λ p A n` | `AmbientLattice.lean` | **Genuine ∞-vol (full)**: convergence, Λ-independence, GKS-I/II, J/h/β monotonicity |

Named specializations at `A = {i}`:
- Finite / Discretized: `magnetization_convergent_{J,h,beta,subgraph}`
- Genuine ∞-vol: `Ambient.magnetizationInfinite`
  (nonneg / le_one / indep_exhaustion / monotone_{J,h,beta} inherited from `correlationInfinite`)

### §4.6: Free energy analyticity and thermodynamic limit

| Result | Statement | File | Regime |
|---|---|---|---|
| `freeEnergy_monotone_{J,h,beta,subgraph}` | monotonicity | `FreeEnergy.lean` | Finite / Discretized Λ↑ |
| `freeEnergy_convergent_subgraph` (Prop 4.6.1) | `f_{Gₙ}` converges | `FreeEnergy.lean` | Discretized Λ↑ |
| `freeEnergyH_analyticOn` (Thm 4.6.2, real) | `f(h)` real-analytic for `h > 0` | `FreeEnergy.lean` | Finite |
| `freeEnergyJ_analyticOn` | `f(J)` real-analytic for `J > 0` | `FreeEnergy.lean` | Finite |
| `partitionFunctionH_analyticAt` | `Z(h)` real-analytic | `FreeEnergy.lean` | Finite |
| `partitionFunctionJ_analyticAt` | `Z(J)` real-analytic | `FreeEnergy.lean` | Finite |
| `isingEdgePoly_nonvanishing_of_graph` | Lee–Yang for Ising partition polynomial | `FreeEnergy.lean` | Finite |
| `sum_eq_map_sup` (§4.6 super-add. prep) | `G ⊕g H = G.map Sum.inl ⊔ H.map Sum.inr` | `SumGraph.lean` | Disjoint-sum lemma |
| `edgeSet_sum` | Edge-set decomposition of `G ⊕g H` | `SumGraph.lean` | Disjoint-sum lemma |
| `disjoint_inl_inr_edgeSet` / `disjoint_inl_inr_edgeFinset` | Set / Finset disjointness of the two images | `SumGraph.lean` | Disjoint-sum lemma |
| `card_edgeFinset_sum` | `#(G ⊕g H).edgeFinset = #G.edgeFinset + #H.edgeFinset` | `SumGraph.lean` | Disjoint-sum lemma |
| `Config.sumEquiv` | `Config (ι ⊕ ι') ≃ Config ι × Config ι'` | `SumModel.lean` | Ising on sum graph |
| `interactionEnergy_sum` / `externalFieldEnergy_sum` | Per-summand additivity of the Hamiltonian's interaction / field contributions on `G ⊕g H` | `SumModel.lean` | Ising on sum graph |
| `hamiltonian_sum` (§4.6 super-add. Step 2-3) | `hamiltonian (G ⊕g H) p (Sum.elim σ₁ σ₂) = hamiltonian G p σ₁ + hamiltonian H p σ₂` | `SumModel.lean` | Ising on sum graph |
| `partitionFunction_sum` (§4.6 super-add. Step 4) | `Z_{G ⊕g H}(p) = Z_G(p) · Z_H(p)` | `SumModel.lean` | Ising on sum graph |
| `log_partitionFunction_sum` | `log Z_{G ⊕g H}(p) = log Z_G(p) + log Z_H(p)` | `SumModel.lean` | Ising on sum graph |
| `partitionFunction_mul_le_of_sum_le` / `log_partitionFunction_add_le_of_sum_le` (§4.6 super-add. Step 5 prep) | `G ⊕g H ≤ G' ⇒ Z_G · Z_H ≤ Z_{G'}` (ferromagnetic), log form | `SumModel.lean` | Ising on sum graph |
| `partitionFunction_map_equiv` / `log_partitionFunction_map_equiv` | `e : V ≃ W ⇒ Z_{G.map e} = Z_G` (iso invariance) | `PartitionFunctionIso.lean` | Step 5 infra |

The bound is uniform in `h ∈ K` but still stage-dependent through `|Λ_n|` and `|E_{Λ_n}|`; it is an envelope for later normalised logarithmic/free-energy estimates, not a standalone stage-uniform Montel bound. ℤ^d wrappers live in `PerStageComplex.lean`. | `AmbientComplexAnalyticity.lean`, `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/PerStageComplex.lean` | ∞-vol Vitali (compact `Z` envelope; PR #2678) |

**Status (as of PR #2729).** The
local-branch form of GJ Thm 4.6.2 finite-volume analyticity is
formalised: `exists_freeEnergyComplex_analyticAt_branch_of_leeYangDomain`
gives, at every `h₀ ∈ leeYangDomain`, an analytic function `f` with
`exp(|ι|·f(h₀)) = Z(h₀)` and `f(h₀) = freeEnergyComplex(h₀)`
(basepoint). Additionally, the Vitali bridge
`vitali_bridge_leeYangDomain` and all modulus bounds
(`norm_partitionFunctionComplex_le_*`,
`norm_freeEnergyComplex_le_trivial_bound`) are in place. The conditional
along-exhaustion Vitali assembly
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_vitali_bridge_leeYangDomain_identified_at_real`
now packages the final handoff from locally uniform convergence to
Lee-Yang holomorphicity and real-axis identification with
`freeEnergyInfinite`. The along-exhaustion per-stage branch wrappers
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_analyticBranch_leeYangDomain_all_stages`
and
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_analyticOnNhd_branch_ball_all_stages`
also package the finite-stage Lee-Yang local branches needed before a
future normal-family argument. The strong ball-level wrappers
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_analyticOnNhd_branch_ball_all_stages_strong`
add basepoint normalisation to the same local witness. The local branch-family
assembly
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_branchFamily_vitali_ball_identified_at_center`
now consumes a locally-uniformly convergent chosen branch family on a ball and
identifies the centre value with `↑freeEnergyInfinite`. The local-cover
handoff
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_branchFamily_vitali_localCover_real`
globalises this conditional statement: if every Lee-Yang point has a local
branch-family witness converging to the same `f`, then `f` is holomorphic on
`leeYangDomain`; under `BoundedEdgeDensity G Λ`,
`DisjointTowerHypotheses G Λ p`, and `(p.h : ℂ) ∈ leeYangDomain`, it also
agrees with `freeEnergyInfinite` at the real centre. PR #2693 adds the
corresponding subsequence-indexed handoff
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_subseq_branchFamily_vitali_localCover_real`:
after a Montel-style extraction with a strictly increasing stage map `σ`, the
same local-cover Vitali and real-axis identification conclusions apply to
branch families whose identities are written at stage `σ m`. The compact-uniform
`Z_ℂ` input
`Ambient.norm_partitionFunctionComplexAlongExhaustion_le_on_isCompact_stage`
now supplies a single real-part bound for all `h` in a compact field set and
all stages of the exhaustion, with the numerical right-hand side still allowed
to depend on the stage. Under bounded edge density and nonvanishing, the upper
normalised-log bridge
`Ambient.exists_real_log_norm_partitionFunctionComplexAlongExhaustion_div_card_le_on_isCompact`
turns it into a stage-independent upper bound for
`Real.log ‖Z_{Λ_n}(h)‖ / |Λ_n|` on compact field sets. On compact subsets of
`leeYangDomain`, `Ambient.exists_log_norm_partitionFunctionComplexAlongExhaustion_div_card_le_leeYangDomain`
uses Lee-Yang nonvanishing to discharge that nonzero hypothesis under
`β > 0`, `J > 0`. This still provides only the upper side of the absolute-log
estimate. The normalised-log bridge
`Ambient.norm_freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_le_of_abs_log_norm_bound_on_set`
then records the conditional passage from a uniform
`|log ‖Z_{Λ_n}(h)‖| / |Λ_n|` bound to the corresponding principal
free-energy bound; deriving that normalised-log bound remains part of the
future normal-family construction. The stage-independent form
`Ambient.norm_freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_le_of_abs_log_norm_bound_on_set_uniform`
packages the resulting locally bounded family as `‖f_n(h)‖ ≤ C + π` on the
same set. PRs #2683-#2691 packaged the Lee-Yang upper/lower-log handoff, the
compact fugacity gap, finite-stage polynomial and `Z_ℂ` lower bounds, the
conditional polynomial-witness lower-log bridge, and compact/ball/point-local
conditional locally bounded wrappers. PR #2692 discharges that remaining
polynomial-witness lower normalised-log input on compact subsets of
`leeYangDomain`: it introduces the one-variable specialization
`MultilinPoly.uniformPolynomial`, proves the root-product estimate
`Polynomial.one_sub_radius_pow_natDegree_le_norm_eval_of_roots_norm_ge_one`,
and applies Lee-Yang nonvanishing to show that the specialized Ising
polynomial has constant value `q(0)=1` and all roots outside the open unit
disk. If the compact fugacity gap gives `‖exp(-2βh)‖ ≤ r < 1` on
`K ⊆ leeYangDomain`, the resulting witness is
`ε_n=(1-r)^|Λ_n|`, with `Real.log ε_n / |Λ_n| = Real.log (1-r)`. Thus
`Ambient.exists_poly_lower_norm_isingEdgePoly_eval_leeYangFugacityVec_on_isCompact`
and
`Ambient.exists_lower_log_norm_partitionFunctionComplexAlongExhaustion_leeYang_of_isCompact`
provide the stage-uniform lower normalised-log bound without an external
polynomial-witness hypothesis. The unconditional locally bounded family
wrappers
`Ambient.exists_norm_freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_le_leeYang_of_isCompact`,
`Ambient.exists_norm_freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_le_leeYang_on_ball`, and
`Ambient.exists_norm_freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_le_leeYang_around`, plus
their ℤ^d wrappers in `PerStageComplex.lean`, now supply the local boundedness
input needed for the remaining normal-family/Vitali branch construction. PRs
#2694-#2696 package the compact-open extraction handoff on one ball, then on
two balls, then on a finite family of Lee-Yang balls, producing one common
strictly increasing subsequence and local differentiable limits under explicit
compact-open compactness hypotheses. PR #2697 adds the explicit-overlap
handoff: if the selected stage branches are eventually equal on every pairwise
overlap, then the extracted local limits agree pairwise on those overlaps. PR
#2698 adds the finite local-patching handoff, patching such compatible local
limits to one differentiable function on the finite union of balls. PR #2699
adds the open-cover patching handoff, replacing the finite-index restriction
with an arbitrary compatible open cover whose union covers `leeYangDomain`.
PR #2700 packages that open-cover handoff for the canonical pointed cover by
Lee-Yang balls indexed by the points of `leeYangDomain`. PR #2701 packages
each point-centred ball's branch-limit witness into
`Ambient.LeeYangLocalBranchLimit` and patches compatible point-indexed
structured data through the same pointed local-cover assembly. PR #2702 adds
the corresponding structured-data real-centre identification wrapper. PR
#2703 packages the data and compatibility into one
`Ambient.LeeYangLocalBranchLimitFamily` endpoint. PR #2704 adds the
real-centred package `Ambient.LeeYangRealBranchLimitFamily`, including
Lee-Yang membership of the real centre and centre normalisation. PR #2705
packages finite compact-open diagonal/overlap output as
`Ambient.LeeYangFiniteSubseqBranchLimitFamily`, with a patching wrapper for the
finite union of balls. PR #2706 adds the corresponding finite packaged
real-centre identification wrapper. PR #2707 composes the compact-open finite
package extraction directly with that real-centre patch. PR #2708 adds the
corresponding compact-open extraction-to-patch wrapper without the real-centre
identification conclusion. PR #2728 connects the pre-Montel
`LeeYangPointwiseNormalisedAllStageBranchData` package to that finite
compact-open extraction and finite patch endpoint for selected Lee-Yang
centres, still under explicit compact-open compactness and overlap-equality
hypotheses. PR #2709 packages finite Lee-Yang cover geometry,
and PR #2729 gives the direct all-stage-data finite Lee-Yang cover bridge using
the radii and Lee-Yang-domain containment already stored in the all-stage
package. PR #2730 adds the all-stage selected real-centre and compact-target
finite-cover patch wrappers, identifying the real-centre value with
`↑freeEnergyInfinite` on compact targets covered by the selected balls.
PR #2731 extracts the selected finite all-stage balls from compactness of
`K`, packages the real-centred `Fin n` geometry, and feeds that geometry back
into the PR #2730 compact real-cover patch bridge.
PR #2710 bundles the selected real-centre index, and PR #2711 adds the compact
target `K` covered by those finite balls.

**Not yet formalized** (future PRs):
- Montel-style locally uniform convergence/subsequence extraction of local
  Lee-Yang branch families from the now-unconditional local boundedness plus
  real-axis Fekete and the branch-family identity theorem; PRs #2694-#2711
  provide post-extraction and conditional patching handoffs through finite
  Lee-Yang covers, finite real-centred cover packages, compact finite targets, arbitrary open covers,
  the pointed Lee-Yang ball cover, and structured local-cover branch-limit
  data, but mathlib still lacks a direct Montel theorem.
- Coherent branch-family construction giving the explicit overlap hypothesis,
  and diagonal coherent local-cover extraction supplying the compatible
  open-cover input.
- Note: the principal-branch `freeEnergyComplex` may be discontinuous
  where `Z` crosses the negative real axis; the local-branch form
  is the mathematically correct statement of GJ Thm 4.6.2.

**Done (liminf form, PR #1643)**: infinite-volume lift of Prop 5.4.2
via `Filter.liminf` of the per-stage `+`-Gibbs expectation. The
canonical ∞-vol `+`-BC quantity
`plusGibbsExpectationLiminf G Λ ⟨J, 0, β⟩ B (fun n σ => sign(σ iₙ))`
satisfies the genuine infinite-volume Peierls bound:
`0 ≤ 1 − plusGibbsExpectationLiminf ≤ exp(-c·β)` under the same
per-stage hypotheses as `prop_5_4_2_along_exhaustion`. Theorems
`prop_5_4_2_plusGibbsExpectationLiminf_lower_bound`,
`prop_5_4_2_plusGibbsExpectationLiminf_bound` (upper),
`prop_5_4_2_plusGibbsExpectationLiminf` (full sandwich) in
`PeierlsInfinite.lean`. The full Gibbs-measure projective-limit
construction with canonical `B` and `i` from the ambient geometry
is deferred (would require additional ambient-geometry machinery
beyond this lift).

### Ambient lattice framework (genuine infinite volume)

`IsingModel/AmbientLattice.lean` introduces the genuine infinite
ambient framework:

| Result | Statement |
|---|---|
| `ConfigOn Λ` | `(↑Λ : Type _) → Spin`, finite-volume configuration type |
| `inducedGraph G Λ` | `SimpleGraph (↑Λ)` induced subgraph |
| `partitionFunctionΛ`, `correlationΛ`, `freeEnergyΛ` | finite-volume objects on `Λ ⊆ V` |
| `partitionFunctionΛ_pos`, `abs_correlationΛ_le_one`, `correlationΛ_le_one`, `correlationΛ_nonneg` | basic properties |
| `Exhaustion V` | structure: monotone `Λₙ` covering any finite set eventually |
| `correlationAlongExhaustion` | correlation along an exhaustion for fixed `A : Finset V` |

| `correlationAlongExhaustion_of_not_subset` | Unfolding helper: `A ⊄ Λ.volume n ⇒ correlationAlongExhaustion n = 0` |
| `abs_correlationAlongExhaustion_eventually_le_one` | eventual boundedness |
| `inducedGraph_mono` | `G₁ ≤ G₂ ⇒ G₁.induce Λ ≤ G₂.induce Λ` |
| `partitionFunctionΛ_monotone_ambient_subgraph` | `G₁ ≤ G₂ ⇒ Z_{G₁,Λ} ≤ Z_{G₂,Λ}` |
| `correlationΛ_monotone_ambient_subgraph` | `G₁ ≤ G₂ ⇒ ⟨σ^A⟩_{G₁,Λ} ≤ ⟨σ^A⟩_{G₂,Λ}` |
| `freeEnergyΛ_monotone_ambient_subgraph` | `G₁ ≤ G₂ ⇒ f_{G₁,Λ} ≤ f_{G₂,Λ}` |

| `extendGraphFromΛ₁_le_induce` | `extendGraphFromΛ₁ G Λ₁ Λ₂ ≤ inducedGraph G Λ₂` |

| `subtypeIncl_injective` | `subtypeIncl` is injective |
| `restrictConfig` | Restrict `(↑Λ₂ → Spin)` to `(↑Λ₁ → Spin)` |

| `truncated3Infinite_h_zero_of_distinct` | `h = 0` + distinct ⇒ `U_3 = 0` (Z₂ symmetry consequence) |

| **`truncated4Infinite`** | **Truncated 4-point correlation** `U_4(i,j,k,l) := ⟨σ^{i,j,k,l}⟩_∞ - ⟨σ^{i,j}⟩_∞⟨σ^{k,l}⟩_∞ - ⟨σ^{i,k}⟩_∞⟨σ^{j,l}⟩_∞ - ⟨σ^{i,l}⟩_∞⟨σ^{j,k}⟩_∞` |
| **`truncated4Infinite_nonpos_h_zero`** | **Lebowitz/U_4 ≤ 0 at infinite volume** (Glimm–Jaffe §4.3 Cor 4.3.3 pp. 68ff): `h = 0` + pairwise distinct ⇒ `U_4 ≤ 0` |
| `truncated4Infinite_indep_exhaustion` | Λ-independence |
| `spontaneousCorrelation_monotone_J` | `MonotoneOn (J ↦ spontaneousCorrelation G Λ J β A) (Ici 0)` |
| `spontaneousCorrelation_monotone_beta` | `MonotoneOn (β ↦ spontaneousCorrelation G Λ J β A) (Ioi 0)` |
| `spontaneousMagnetization_monotone_J` | Singleton specialization at `A = {i}` |
| `spontaneousMagnetization_monotone_beta` | Singleton specialization at `A = {i}` |

## Axioms

All four axioms are in `Inequalities/GHS.lean` and `ContinuousSpin/Phi4.lean`.
They are mathematically proved (and documented) but formalization requires
heavy measure theory setup:

- `phi4_single_site_nonneg`: non-negativity of the symmetrized 4D
  integral (`ContinuousSpin/Phi4.lean`)
- `lebowitz_third`: 3-site Lebowitz inequality for continuous `φ⁴`,
  transferred to Ising via the `λ → ∞` limit
- `lebowitz_four`: 4-site version, same route
- `lebowitz_inductive`: inductive form of Cor 4.3.2

| §4.6 | **Prop 4.6.1 concrete toolkit — named limits, sandwich, invariances, cross-family equivalences, monotonicity** | **Done (PRs #646–#663)** | Building on the four concrete ℤ^d Fekete applications (linearBox / stripeBrick2D / slabBrick / centeredSlab), PRs #646–#663 assemble a complete toolkit for reasoning about the infinite-volume free-energy density limits. **Named limits** (`Classical.choose`): `freeEnergyInfinite_linearBox`, `_stripeBrick2D`, `_slabBrick`, `_centeredSlab` (PRs #646/#648). **J=0 closed form**: all four `freeEnergyInfinite_* ⟨0, h, β⟩ = log(2·cosh(β·h))` under ferromagnetic `0 ≤ h, 0 < β` (PRs #647/#648). **Infinite-volume sandwich**: `log 2 ≤ freeEnergyInfinite_* ≤ log 2 + |β|·((d+1)·|J| + |h|)` (PR #650). **Tighter cosh lower bound**: `log(2·cosh(β·h)) ≤ freeEnergyInfinite_*` transporting `freeEnergy_ge_log_two_cosh` via `ge_of_tendsto` (PR #659); combined cosh-form sandwich (PR #660). **Nonnegativity**: `0 ≤ freeEnergyInfinite_*` via `freeEnergy_nonneg_of_ferromagnetic` (PR #653). **Translation invariance of the Fekete limit**: `freeEnergyInfinite_*_tendsto_shift` — any coord-shift of the slab sequence converges to the same named limit (PR #652). **Cross-family equivalences**: `freeEnergyInfinite_linearBox = freeEnergyInfinite_slabBrick Fin.elim0`, `_stripeBrick2D = _slabBrick (fun _ => w)` (PR #651), `_centeredSlab = _slabBrick` via `shift_(-n) (slabBrick widths (2n))` + `n ↦ 2n` subsequence convergence (PRs #654/#655), direct transitive identity `_centeredSlab (d=1) = _stripeBrick2D` (PR #656). **Full three-parameter monotonicity** via `le_of_tendsto_of_tendsto'` on per-stage `freeEnergy_monotone_{β,h,J}`: β-monotonicity on `Ioi 0` (PR #661), h- and J-monotonicity on `Ici 0` (PR #662). **Fekete at any real h via |h|-symmetry**: `freeEnergy_*_tendsto_of_abs_h` — for ferromagnetic `J ≥ 0, β > 0` and any real h, the sequence at `⟨J, h, β⟩` converges to `freeEnergyInfinite_* ⟨J, |h|, β⟩` via `freeEnergy_eq_abs_h` even-symmetry (PR #663). All four `freeEnergyInfinite_*` are now fully related in the matching-widths slice of the parameter grid. |
| §4.6 | **Two-sided centered slab on `latticeGraph (d+1)` (strip-Fekete form)** | **Done (definitions + combinatorics + super-additivity + BddAbove + Tendsto)** | `IsingModel.Concrete.centeredSlab (widths : Fin d → ℕ) (n : ℕ) : Finset (Fin (d+1) → ℤ)` (`Concrete/CenteredSlab.lean`) — two-sided slab `[-n, n) × ∏_{j : Fin d} [0, widths j)` centered at the origin on coord 0 (cardinality `2n · ∏ widths j`, linear in `n`). Complements the single-sided slab of PR #642 with a symmetric `[-n, n)` range. Note: *not* a van Hove / Følner sequence in the full ambient `ℤ^(d+1)` — with fixed transverse widths, the boundary in fixed directions is `Θ(n)`, same order as the volume; the statement here is strip/slab convergence, not full-lattice van Hove. Combinatorics: `centeredSlab_card` (= 2n · ∏ widths j), `centeredSlab_card_add`, `mem_centeredSlab`, `centeredSlab_one_card_ne_zero` (under `∀ j, widths j ≠ 0`). **Double-shift decomposition**: `centeredSlab_disjoint_double_shift` / `centeredSlab_union_double_shift` — `centeredSlab (m+n) = shift_(-n) (centeredSlab m) ∪ shift_m (centeredSlab n)`, corresponding to the interval identity `[-m-n, m-n) ∪ [m-n, m+n) = [-m-n, m+n)`. The two halves meet at the coord-0 midpoint `m - n`. **Super-additivity**: `log_partitionFunctionΛ_centeredSlab_super_additive` — Fekete argument via disjoint-union super-additivity + translation invariance applied to **both** shifted halves + subsingleton transport. **BddAbove**: `centeredSlab_freeEnergy_le` / `freeEnergy_centeredSlab_bddAbove` — same uniform bound `log 2 + |β|·((d+1)·|J| + |h|)`. **Fekete convergence**: `freeEnergy_centeredSlab_tendsto`. First Fekete-compatible concrete realization of a two-sided (van-Hove-style) exhaustion. (PR #644.) |

| §4.6 | ℤ^d two-point function (Finset-based, depends on separation) | **Done** | `twoPointFunction (d : ℕ) (p : IsingParams ℝ) (r : Fin d → ℤ) : ℝ := correlationInfinite (latticeGraph d) (cubicExhaustion d) p {0, r}` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation.lean`). Identity `correlationInfinite_latticeGraph_pair_eq_twoPointFunction`: `correlationInfinite ... {i, j} = twoPointFunction d p (j - i)` (ferromagnetic). Symmetry `twoPointFunction_symm`: `twoPointFunction d p r = twoPointFunction d p (-r)` (via unordered pair + translation). Caveat: at `r = 0`, `{0, 0}` collapses to `{0}`, so `twoPointFunction d p 0 = magnetizationInfinite ... 0`, not the physical `⟨σ_0^2⟩ = 1` (same Finset caveat as `susceptibility_J_zero`). For `r ≠ 0` this matches the physical `⟨σ_0 σ_r⟩_∞`. |

Identity `truncated2Infinite_latticeGraph_cubicExhaustion_eq_twoPoint`: `truncated2Infinite ... p i j = truncated2TwoPoint d p (j - i)` (ferromagnetic). Symmetry `truncated2TwoPoint_symm` via `truncated2Infinite_symm` + translation. Consequence of PR #256 + `truncated2Infinite_symm`. |

ℤ^d wrappers live in `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/PerStageComplex.lean`. The required normalised-log control remains the missing normal-family input. (PR #2679.) |

Since nonempty finite stages have `1 ≤ |Λ_n|`, the PR #2679 bound improves to the stage-independent estimate `‖f_n(h)‖ ≤ C + π` on the same set. ℤ^d wrapper: `_latticeGraph` in `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/PerStageComplex.lean`. This is the locally bounded family shape needed by Montel, still conditional on the normalised-log hypothesis. (PR #2680.) |

| §5.1 | Cluster property — `J = 0` (non-interacting) slice (pp. 72–74) | **Done (trivial slice)** | `truncated2_J_zero_of_ne` (`Inequalities/GHS.lean`): for `i ≠ j` and any `h, β`, `truncated2 G ⟨0, h, β⟩ i j = 0`. Via `correlation_J_zero` (`⟨σ^A⟩ = tanh(β·h)^{\|A\|}`) + `Finset.card_pair`/`card_singleton`. The non-interacting slice: the Hamiltonian has no `J`-coupling, so any two distinct sites factorise identically — no distance / separation / high-temperature hypothesis needed (`β` is arbitrary). General-`J` decay-at-large-separation in pure phases remains unformalized. |
| §5.1 | Cluster property — `β = 0` (infinite-temperature) slice (pp. 72–74) | **Done (trivial slice)** | `truncated2_beta_zero` (`Inequalities/GHS.lean`): for any `J, h` and any sites `i, j` (not necessarily distinct), `truncated2 G ⟨J, h, 0⟩ i j = 0`. Via `correlation_beta_zero_vanish_of_nonempty_A`. Companion to the `J = 0` slice. At `β = 0` the diagonal truncated value also vanishes since `⟨σ_i⟩ = 0`. |
| §5.1 | Cluster property — truncated 3-point trivial slices (pp. 72–74) | **Done (trivial slices)** | `truncated3_J_zero_of_pairwise_distinct` and `truncated3_beta_zero` (`Inequalities/GHS.lean`): the Ursell 3-point function vanishes at `J = 0` (pairwise distinct sites, via `correlation_J_zero` giving the `t³ - 3t³ + 2t³ = 0` combination with `t = tanh(β·h)`) and at `β = 0` (any sites; coincident indices are collapsed at the Finset level in the definition of `truncated3`, so the statement is unconditional on distinctness — cf. `truncated2_beta_zero`). Extension of `truncated2_*` lemmas to the 3-point case. `correlation_beta_zero_vanish_of_nonempty_A` is the common ingredient at `β = 0`. |
| §5.1 | Cluster property — ∞-vol J=0 slice (pp. 72–74) | **Done (trivial slice ∞-vol lift)** | `correlationInfinite_J_zero` (`AmbientLattice.lean`): for ferromagnetic `⟨0, h, β⟩`, `correlationInfinite G Λ ⟨0, h, β⟩ A = tanh(β·h)^{\|A\|}`. Proof via `correlationAlongExhaustion_J_zero_of_subset` (stagewise closed form using `liftFinset_card` helper) + `correlationAlongExhaustion_J_zero_eventually_eq` (eventually-constant sequence) + uniqueness of limits against `correlationAlongExhaustion_tendsto_ciSup`. Corollary: `truncated2Infinite_J_zero_of_ne` — ∞-vol counterpart of `truncated2_J_zero_of_ne` (PR #207). |
| §5.1 | Cluster property — ∞-vol β=0 slice (pp. 72–74) | **Done (trivial slice ∞-vol lift)** | `correlationInfinite_beta_zero_vanish` (`AmbientLattice.lean`): for any `J, h` and nonempty `A`, `correlationInfinite G Λ ⟨J, h, 0⟩ A = 0`. Proof: the sequence `correlationAlongExhaustion G Λ ⟨J, h, 0⟩ A` is pointwise zero (`correlation_beta_zero_vanish_of_nonempty_A` on the lifted subset when `A ⊆ Λ.volume n`; default-0 otherwise), hence `⨆ = 0`. No ferromagnetic hypothesis needed. Corollary: `truncated2Infinite_beta_zero` — ∞-vol counterpart of `truncated2_beta_zero` (PR #208). |
| §5.1 | Cluster property — ∞-vol truncated 3-point trivial slices (pp. 72–74) | **Done (trivial slices ∞-vol lift)** | `truncated3Infinite_J_zero_of_pairwise_distinct` and `truncated3Infinite_beta_zero` (`AmbientLattice.lean`): ∞-vol counterparts of the finite-volume PR #209 slices. J=0 ferromagnetic case via `correlationInfinite_J_zero` + Finset card identities (`t³ - 3·t³ + 2·t³ = 0`). β=0 case via `correlationInfinite_beta_zero_vanish` (all seven Ursell terms zero). |
| §5.1 | Cluster property — Lebowitz 4-point β=0 slice (finite + ∞-vol) (pp. 72–74) | **Done (β=0 trivial slice)** | `truncated4_beta_zero` (`Inequalities/GHS.lean`) and `truncated4Infinite_beta_zero` (`AmbientLattice.lean`): at β=0 the Lebowitz 4-point truncated function vanishes identically, via `correlation_beta_zero_vanish_of_nonempty_A` / `correlationInfinite_beta_zero_vanish`. Note: J=0 does *not* give vanishing (for pairwise distinct sites the Lebowitz 4-point is `-2·t⁴` with `t = tanh(β·h)`), so only the β=0 slice is added.
| §5.1 | Cluster property — Lebowitz 4-point J=0 closed form (finite + ∞-vol) (pp. 72–74) | **Done (J=0 closed form)** | `truncated4_J_zero_of_pairwise_distinct` (`Inequalities/GHS.lean`) and `truncated4Infinite_J_zero_of_pairwise_distinct` (`AmbientLattice.lean`, ferromagnetic): at J=0 with pairwise distinct sites, the Lebowitz 4-point equals `-2·t⁴` with `t = tanh(β·h)`. Via `correlation_J_zero` / `correlationInfinite_J_zero` + Finset card identities (4, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2). This is non-vanishing in general but always `≤ 0`, consistent with Cor 4.3.3's `U₄ ≤ 0` bound at h=0 (the J=0 slice here is a separate special case, not a direct witness). |
| §5.1 | Cluster property — conditional decay: summable ⇒ cofinite → 0 (pp. 72–74) | **Done (conditional real-analysis step)** | `truncated2Infinite_tendsto_cofinite_zero_of_summable` (`AmbientLattice.lean`): `Summable (fun j : V => truncated2Infinite G Λ p i j) → Tendsto ... Filter.cofinite (nhds 0)`. The `Summable` hypothesis is a finiteness condition on the two-point function summed over `j`; in translation-invariant / connected-correlation settings it matches the physical "finite susceptibility" (away from the critical line). `Filter.cofinite` is the filter of cofinite subsets; on `Fin d → ℤ` (with `d ≥ 1`) bounded subsets are finite, so cofinite aligns with the informal "|r| → ∞" filter. Direct specialization of mathlib's `Summable.tendsto_cofinite_zero`. ℤ^d wrapper `truncated2Infinite_latticeGraph_tendsto_cofinite_zero_of_summable` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/CorrelationDecay.lean`). This PR supplies the elementary real-analysis building block; later Simon–Lieb/high-temperature results discharge the summability input in high-temperature regimes. (PR #779.) |
| §5.1 | ℓ¹ lattice distance on ℤ^d (infrastructure for cluster decay) | **Done (definition + 5 basic lemmas)** | `latticeDistance (d : ℕ) (x y : Fin d → ℤ) : ℕ := ∑ i, (x i - y i).natAbs` (`Lattice.lean`). Basic lemmas: `latticeDistance_self` (`= 0`), `latticeDistance_comm` (symmetry), `latticeDistance_eq_zero_iff` (`= 0 ↔ x = y`), `latticeDistance_triangle` (`d(x,z) ≤ d(x,y) + d(y,z)`), and `latticeGraph_adj_iff_latticeDistance_eq_one` (`(latticeGraph d).Adj x y ↔ latticeDistance d x y = 1`). Range kept in `ℕ` to sidestep `ℕ`/`ℤ`/`ℝ` cast bookkeeping in subsequent summability arguments. Part of the §5.1 cluster-decay infrastructure series (Epic #780); the companion statement that `Filter.cofinite` on `Fin d → ℤ` is equivalent to `latticeDistance (fixed basepoint, ·) → ∞` for `d ≥ 1` (which upgrades PR #779 into a concrete distance-based statement) is deferred to a follow-up PR. (PR #781.) |
| §5.1 | Finite ℓ¹ balls + proper map to ℕ (infrastructure for cluster decay) | **Done (finite balls + `Tendsto` to `atTop` along `cofinite`)** | `latticeDistance_le_finite` + `tendsto_latticeDistance_atTop_cofinite` (`Lattice.lean`). The ball `{j : Fin d → ℤ | latticeDistance d i j ≤ N}` is finite for every dimension `d`, basepoint `i`, and radius `N`, via the coordinatewise bound `(i k - j k).natAbs ≤ latticeDistance d i j` (one summand is at most a ℕ-sum) placing each `j k` in a finite integer interval `[i k - N, i k + N]`, then `Set.Finite.pi` + `Set.finite_Icc`. The proper-map property `Tendsto (fun j => latticeDistance d i j) Filter.cofinite Filter.atTop` follows by reducing `Filter.eventually_cofinite` to finiteness of `{j | latticeDistance d i j < N}` for every `N`, a subset of the ball at radius `N`. No `d ≥ 1` hypothesis (for `d = 0` the domain `Fin 0 → ℤ` is a singleton, `Filter.cofinite` collapses to `⊥`, and the Tendsto holds vacuously). Combined with PR #779's conditional cofinite cluster decay this is the upgrade pathway to a distance-based "`U_2 → 0` as `d_latt(i,j) → ∞`" statement. (PR #782.) |
| §5.1 | Distance-based cluster decay capstone (compose PR #779 + PR #782) | **Done (conditional, distance-based form)** | `comap_latticeDistance_atTop_eq_cofinite` (`Lattice.lean`): `Filter.comap (fun j => latticeDistance d i j) Filter.atTop = Filter.cofinite`. The `≥` direction is `Filter.Tendsto.le_comap` of PR #782's `tendsto_latticeDistance_atTop_cofinite`; the `≤` direction uses `Set.Finite.bddAbove` on `ℕ` to bound `latticeDistance d i` on the finite complement of any cofinite set, then takes `Set.Ici (M+1) ∈ Filter.atTop`. ℤ^d capstone `truncated2Infinite_latticeGraph_tendsto_atTop_zero_of_summable` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/CorrelationDecay.lean`): under summability of `j ↦ U_2(i, j)`, `Tendsto (fun j => truncated2Infinite (latticeGraph d) Λ p i j) (Filter.comap (fun j => latticeDistance d i j) Filter.atTop) (nhds 0)`. Equivalent ε-N reading: for every `ε > 0` there exists `N : ℕ` such that `latticeDistance d i j ≥ N` implies `|U_2(i, j)| < ε`. The proof is a one-line rewrite of the comap filter via the equality lemma followed by PR #779's cofinite version. The capstone is a `Summable`-conditioned corollary, not a standalone GJ result: it presents the §5.1 cluster picture in its distance-based form, with the `Summable` hypothesis serving as the conditional input later discharged in high-temperature regimes by the Simon–Lieb stack (FV Prop 9.31). Together with PRs #779 / #781 / #782 this completes the conditional §5.1 cluster-decay infrastructure stack. (PR #783.) |
| §5.1 | `LocallyFinite (latticeGraph d)` + ambient degree ≤ 2d (Simon–Lieb prep) | **Done (ambient `Fintype` instance + degree bound)** | `latticeGraph_neighborSet_fintype` + `latticeGraph_degree_le` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`). Provides `Fintype ((latticeGraph d).neighborSet v)` for every `v : Fin d → ℤ`, i.e. `LocallyFinite (latticeGraph d)`, via `Fintype.ofFinset` on `(latticeNeighborEnum d v).filter ((latticeGraph d).Adj v)` together with `latticeGraph_adj_mem_neighborEnum`. The degree bound `(latticeGraph d).degree v ≤ 2 * d` follows from `Finset.card_le_card` of the inclusion `neighborFinset v ⊆ latticeNeighborEnum d v` and the existing `latticeNeighborEnum_card_le`. Until now the project's `Fintype` reasoning all happened on the induced subgraph `(inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) Λ)` for finite `Λ`; this PR is the first time the unrestricted ambient `latticeGraph d` is given a `LocallyFinite` instance, unlocking the standard `neighborFinset` / `degree` API and making the next infrastructure layer toward FV Prop 9.31 (outer vertex boundary, edge boundary, etc.) statable in mathlib's standard idiom. (PR #784.) |
| §5.1 | Outer vertex boundary `∂_o^v S` of a Finset (Simon–Lieb prep step 2) | **Done (generic def + 4 lemmas + ℤ^d card bound)** | `SimpleGraph.outerVertexBoundary G S := S.biUnion G.neighborFinset \ S` (placed in mathlib's `SimpleGraph` namespace, `Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`) for any `[LocallyFinite G] [DecidableEq V]`. Lemmas: `mem_outerVertexBoundary_iff` (`y ∈ ∂_o^v S ↔ y ∉ S ∧ ∃ x ∈ S, G.Adj x y`), `outerVertexBoundary_disjoint` (`Disjoint (∂_o^v S) S`), `outerVertexBoundary_empty` (`∂_o^v ∅ = ∅`), `outerVertexBoundary_card_le_sum_degrees` (`|∂_o^v S| ≤ ∑ x ∈ S, G.degree x` via `Finset.sdiff_subset` + `Finset.card_biUnion_le` + `card_neighborFinset_eq_degree`). ℤ^d wrapper `IsingModel.Ambient.latticeGraph_outerVertexBoundary_card_le`: `((latticeGraph d).outerVertexBoundary S).card ≤ 2 * d * S.card`, combining the generic sum-of-degrees bound with PR #784's `latticeGraph_degree_le`. Together with PR #784 this exposes the standard outer-vertex-boundary indexing object as preparation for the eventual Simon–Lieb statement (FV Prop 9.31, Epic #780 B-iii). (PR #785.) |
| §5.1 | Inner vertex boundary `∂_i^v S` + outer-by-inner degree bound (Simon–Lieb prep step 3) | **Done (generic def + 3 lemmas + outer-by-inner bound + ℤ^d wrapper)** | `SimpleGraph.innerVertexBoundary G S := S.filter (fun x => ¬ (G.neighborFinset x ⊆ S))` (mathlib's `SimpleGraph` namespace, `Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`) for `[LocallyFinite G] [DecidableEq V]`. Lemmas: `mem_innerVertexBoundary_iff` (`x ∈ ∂_i^v S ↔ x ∈ S ∧ ∃ y, G.Adj x y ∧ y ∉ S` via `Finset.not_subset` + `mem_neighborFinset`), `innerVertexBoundary_subset_self` (`⊆ S` via `Finset.filter_subset`), `innerVertexBoundary_empty` (`∂_i^v ∅ = ∅`). Substantive outer-by-inner boundary degree bound `outerVertexBoundary_card_le_sum_degrees_innerVertexBoundary`: `(∂_o^v S).card ≤ ∑ x ∈ ∂_i^v S, G.degree x` — every outer-boundary vertex `y` is reached as a neighbour of some inner-boundary vertex `x` (the witness for `y ∈ ∂_o^v S` has the outside neighbour `y`, hence sits in `∂_i^v S`); `outerVertexBoundary G S ⊆ (innerVertexBoundary G S).biUnion neighborFinset`, then `Finset.card_biUnion_le` + `card_neighborFinset_eq_degree`. The outer boundary is upper-bounded by a sum of degrees ranging only over the inner boundary. ℤ^d wrapper `latticeGraph_outerVertexBoundary_card_le_two_mul_d_mul_innerVertexBoundary_card`: `((latticeGraph d).∂_o^v S).card ≤ 2d · ((latticeGraph d).∂_i^v S).card` — the elementary max-degree-based linear bound on `ℤ^d` (not the optimal vertex-isoperimetric inequality). (PR #786.) |
| §5.1 | Oriented edge boundary `∂^e S` of a Finset (Simon–Lieb prep step 4) | **Done (generic def + 3 lemmas + ℤ^d card bound)** | `SimpleGraph.edgeBoundary G S : Finset (V × V)` := `(innerVertexBoundary G S).biUnion (fun x => ((neighborFinset x).filter (· ∉ S)).image (fun y => (x, y)))` (mathlib's `SimpleGraph` namespace, `Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`) for `[LocallyFinite G] [DecidableEq V]`. Each crossing edge is recorded exactly once with its `S`-endpoint listed first, sidestepping the `Sym2 V` quotient handling. Lemmas: `mem_edgeBoundary_iff` (`(x, y) ∈ ∂^e S ↔ x ∈ S ∧ y ∉ S ∧ G.Adj x y` via `Finset.mem_biUnion` + `mem_image` + `mem_filter` + `mem_innerVertexBoundary_iff` + `Prod.mk.injEq`), `edgeBoundary_empty` (`∂^e ∅ = ∅` from `innerVertexBoundary_empty`), `edgeBoundary_card_le_sum_degrees_innerVertexBoundary` (`(∂^e S).card ≤ ∑ x ∈ ∂_i^v S, G.degree x` via `Finset.card_biUnion_le` + `card_image_le` + `card_filter_le` + `card_neighborFinset_eq_degree`). ℤ^d wrapper `latticeGraph_edgeBoundary_card_le_two_mul_d_mul_innerVertexBoundary_card`: `((latticeGraph d).∂^e S).card ≤ 2d · ((latticeGraph d).∂_i^v S).card`. Together with PRs #785 / #786 this closes the elementary boundary-primitive infrastructure (outer/inner vertex + oriented edge) for any locally finite simple graph; subsequent Simon-Lieb work (Epic #780 B-iii) is research-level. (PR #787.) |
| §5.1 | Edge boundary card disjoint biUnion equality (≤ → =) | **Done (PR #787 bound strengthened to equality)** | `SimpleGraph.edgeBoundary_card_eq_sum_inner_filter` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`): `(edgeBoundary G S).card = ∑ x ∈ innerVertexBoundary G S, ((neighborFinset x).filter (· ∉ S)).card`. The `biUnion` defining `edgeBoundary` is disjoint across `x ∈ ∂_i^v S` (different first coordinates), so `Finset.card_biUnion` applies; each summand's image cardinality equals the filter's via `Finset.card_image_of_injective` (`y ↦ (x, y)` injective in `y`). Bounding each filter cardinality by `(neighborFinset x).card = G.degree x` recovers PR #787's inequality `edgeBoundary_card_le_sum_degrees_innerVertexBoundary`. (PR #788.) |
| §5.1 | Edge boundary card outer-side equality (companion of PR #788) | **Done (double-counting identity completed)** | `SimpleGraph.edgeBoundary_card_eq_sum_outer_filter` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`): `(edgeBoundary G S).card = ∑ y ∈ outerVertexBoundary G S, ((neighborFinset y).filter (· ∈ S)).card` — counts crossing edges by their outside endpoint. Proof: rewrite `edgeBoundary G S` as a disjoint `biUnion` indexed by `y ∈ ∂_o^v S` via `Finset.ext` (forward / backward use `mem_edgeBoundary_iff`, `mem_outerVertexBoundary_iff`, and `G.symm` to swap adjacency), then `Finset.card_biUnion` (disjointness across distinct second coordinates) + `Finset.card_image_of_injective` (`x ↦ (x, y)` injective in `x`). Combined with PR #788's inner-side equality this yields the double-counting identity `∑ x ∈ ∂_i^v S, |N(x) \ S| = ∑ y ∈ ∂_o^v S, |N(y) ∩ S|`. (PR #789.) |
| §5.1 | Vertex-by-edge boundary bounds (`|∂_i^v|, |∂_o^v| ≤ |∂^e|`) | **Done (boundary calculus rounded out)** | `SimpleGraph.innerVertexBoundary_card_le_edgeBoundary_card` and `SimpleGraph.outerVertexBoundary_card_le_edgeBoundary_card` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`). Each `x ∈ ∂_i^v S` has a witness neighbour `y ∉ S` from `mem_innerVertexBoundary_iff`, so the filter `(N(x)).filter (· ∉ S)` has cardinality `≥ 1`; sum monotonicity from PR #788's `edgeBoundary_card_eq_sum_inner_filter` then gives `|∂_i^v S| ≤ |∂^e S|`. Symmetric proof for the outer-side bound via `mem_outerVertexBoundary_iff`, `G.symm`, and PR #789's `edgeBoundary_card_eq_sum_outer_filter`. Together with the existing edge-boundary bounds these give the linear chain `|∂_i^v|, |∂_o^v| ≤ |∂^e| ≤ deg_max · |∂_i^v|` (on `latticeGraph d` the rightmost is `2d · |∂_i^v|`, PR #787 + #784). (PR #790.) |
| §5.1 | Edge boundary outer-side degree bound + ℤ^d wrapper (companion of PR #787) | **Done (boundary calculus made symmetric)** | `SimpleGraph.edgeBoundary_card_le_sum_degrees_outerVertexBoundary`: `(edgeBoundary G S).card ≤ ∑ y ∈ outerVertexBoundary G S, G.degree y` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`), via PR #789's `edgeBoundary_card_eq_sum_outer_filter` + per-summand `Finset.card_filter_le` + `card_neighborFinset_eq_degree`. ℤ^d wrapper `latticeGraph_edgeBoundary_card_le_two_mul_d_mul_outerVertexBoundary_card`: `((latticeGraph d).edgeBoundary S).card ≤ 2d · ((latticeGraph d).outerVertexBoundary S).card`, combining the generic outer-side bound with PR #784's `latticeGraph_degree_le`. Together with the inner-side bound (PR #787) gives the strictly stronger combined bound `|∂^e S| ≤ 2d · min(|∂_i^v S|, |∂_o^v S|)` on `latticeGraph d`. (PR #791.) |
| §5.1 | **Random current foundation (Simon-Lieb attempt step 1, Issue #780 multi-PR start)** | **Foundation (Current type + Zero/Add)** | `IsingModel.Ambient.Current G Λ` (`RandomCurrent.lean`): `(inducedGraph G Λ).edgeSet → ℕ`, the type of ℕ-valued currents on a finite induced subgraph used in the random-current representation of the Ising 2-point function (FV §3.7, Prop 9.31). Instances: `Current.instZero` (constant zero), `Current.instAdd` (pointwise addition). First PR in the §5.1 Simon-Lieb multi-PR series; subsequent PRs will add parity at vertex, source/sink characterisation, the switching lemma, and the random-current representation `⟨σ_A⟩^Λ = (current sum) / Z`. (PR #796.) |
| §5.1 | **Random current expression of the Ising correlation function (unconditional capstone) + N → ∞ limit chain + switching prep** (multi-PR series, Issue #780) | **Done (20 PRs: Layer 4 LHS+RHS limit, Layer 5 monotone-convergence + uniform bound + unconditional capstone, switching prep primitives)** | **Capstone (PR #865, unconditional)**: `CurrentBounded.pow_card_mul_iSup_weightSum_eq_sum_spinA_prod_exp` (`RandomCurrent.lean`): for `0 ≤ β J`, `2^|Λ| · (⨆_N CurrentBounded.weightSum N A β J) = ∑_σ σ_A · ∏_e Real.exp (β J σ_e)` — the random-current expression of the Ising correlation function `Z · ⟨σ_A⟩` (FV §3.7 eq. (3.45)) **without any external `Summable`/`BddAbove` hypothesis**. **Layer 4 (LHS+RHS N→∞ limit, PRs #850-#859)**: `Real.exp_eq_tsum_div_factorial`, `Real.tendsto_partial_sum_atTop_exp`, `Config.tendsto_prod_Fin_partial_sum_atTop_prod_exp`, `_sum_spinA_prod_partial_sum_..._sum_spinA_prod_exp`, `_pow_card_mul_currentBounded_weightSum_atTop_sum_spinA_prod_exp` (LHS capstone); `Current.boundedFinset` + `mem_boundedFinset_iff` + `boundedFinset_mono` + `exists_mem_boundedFinset` (cofinality) + `tendsto_boundedFinset_atTop_finsetAtTop` + `Summable.tendsto_sum_boundedFinset` + `CurrentBounded.weightSum_eq_sum_boundedFinset` + `CurrentBounded.tendsto_weightSum_atTop_currentWeightSum` (RHS capstone, summable hyp). **Layer 5 (monotone-convergence + uniform bound + unconditional, PRs #860-#865)**: `CurrentBounded.weightSum_mono` (under `0 ≤ β J`) + `_tendsto_weightSum_atTop_iSup` (BddAbove + `tendsto_atTop_ciSup`) + `Real.partial_sum_le_exp_of_nonneg` (per-edge bound) + `CurrentBounded.weightSum_le_exp_pow_card` (uniform N-independent bound `≤ exp(βJ)^|edgeSet|`) + `_tendsto_weightSum_atTop_iSup_of_nonneg` (unconditional, BddAbove discharged). **Switching prep (PRs #866-#867)**: `Current.le_def` (Pi LE pointwise) + `Current.zero_le` + `Current.le_self_add_right`/`_left` + `Current.subFinset n` (Finset of `m ≤ n` via `Fintype.piFinset (Finset.range (n e + 1))`) + `Current.mem_subFinset_iff` + `Current.subFinset_card_eq_prod` (`= ∏ e, (n e + 1)`); the parameterizing set for the Aizenman pair-bijection `{(n₁, n₂) : n₁ + n₂ = n} ↔ {m : m ≤ n}` (Aizenman 1982 Lemma 4.1 / FV §3.7). Truncation: `Current.instSub` (Pi pointwise via Nat.sub) + `Current.sub_apply` + `Current.sub_add_cancel_of_le` (`(n-m)+m = n`, name aligns with `Nat.sub_add_cancel`) + `Current.add_sub_cancel_of_le` (commuted: `m+(n-m) = n`) + `Current.sub_le_self` (n-m ≤ n). Pair-Finset (PR #868): `Current.pairFinset n : Finset (Current G Λ × Current G Λ)` (= `(subFinset n).image (m ↦ (m, n - m))`) + `mem_pairFinset_iff` (`(m₁, m₂) ∈ pairFinset n ↔ m₁ + m₂ = n`) + `pairFinset_card_eq_subFinset_card` (image of injective fn) + `pairFinset_card_eq_prod` (`= ∏ e, (n e + 1)`); the LHS Finset of the Aizenman switching pair-bijection. Pair-weight scaling (PR #869): `Current.sum_pairFinset_eq_sum_subFinset` (`∑ p ∈ pairFinset n, f p = ∑ m ∈ subFinset n, f (m, n - m)`, sum reindexing via `Finset.sum_image`) + `Current.sum_pairFinset_weight_mul_weight` (`∑ p ∈ pairFinset n, weight β J p.1 * weight β J p.2 = weight β J n * ∑ m ∈ subFinset n, jointFactor m (n - m)`, the central scaling identity combining sum reindexing + `weight_mul_weight_eq_weight_add_mul_jointFactor` (PR #845) + `add_sub_cancel_of_le` (PR #867)). Sub source-set algebra (PR #870): `Current.sub_sources_eq_symmDiff` (`m ≤ n → (n - m).sources = symmDiff (sources n) (sources m)`, via `sub_add_cancel_of_le` + `add_sources_eq` + symmDiff involution) + `Current.sub_hasSources_iff` (`m ≤ n → (n - m).HasSources A ↔ symmDiff sources_n sources_m = A`). Sub trivial algebra (PR #871): `Current.sub_zero` (`n - 0 = n`) + `Current.zero_sub` (`0 - n = 0`, truncated) + `Current.sub_self` (`n - n = 0`) + `Current.subFinset_zero` (`subFinset 0 = {0}`). Pair-Finset boundary cases (PR #872): `Current.zero_mem_pairFinset` (`(0, n) ∈ pairFinset n`) + `Current.self_mem_pairFinset` (`(n, 0) ∈ pairFinset n`) + `Current.pairFinset_zero` (`pairFinset 0 = {(0, 0)}`). Sub_sub_self + compl involution (PR #873): `Current.sub_sub_self_of_le` (`m ≤ n → n - (n - m) = m`) + `Current.subFinset_image_compl` (`(subFinset n).image (m ↦ n - m) = subFinset n`); the natural involution on subFinset induced by pair swap. Pair swap + nonempty (PR #874): `Current.pairFinset_image_swap_eq_self` ((pairFinset n).image Prod.swap = pairFinset n, by add_comm) + `Current.pairFinset_nonempty` ((pairFinset n).Nonempty since (n, 0) ∈ pairFinset n). JointFactor closed form (PR #875): `Current.jointFactor_compl_eq_prod_choose` (m ≤ n → jointFactor m (n - m) = ∏ e, C(n e, m e)) + `Current.sum_subFinset_jointFactor_compl_eq_pow_two` (∑ m ∈ subFinset n, jointFactor m (n - m) = 2^(∑ e, n e), via `Finset.prod_univ_sum` Fubini + `Nat.sum_range_choose` (binomial row sum) + `Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum`). Pair-weight closed form capstone (PR #876): `Current.sum_pairFinset_weight_mul_weight_eq_weight_pow_two` (`∑ p ∈ pairFinset n, weight β J p.1 * weight β J p.2 = weight β J n * 2^(∑ e, n e)`, combining PR #869 + PR #875). Source-conditioned pair Finset (PR #877): `Current.pairFinset_with_sources n A B` (filter of `pairFinset n` by `n₁.HasSources A ∧ n₂.HasSources B`) + `mem_pairFinset_with_sources_iff` + `pairFinset_with_sources_eq_empty_of_sources_mismatch` (when symmDiff A B ≠ sources n). Source-conditioned subFinset (PR #878): `Current.subFinset_with_source n A` (filter of `subFinset n` by `m.HasSources A`) + `mem_subFinset_with_source_iff` + `subFinset_with_source_subset`; dual to pairFinset_with_sources via pair-bijection. Pair ↔ subFinset bridge (PR #879): `Current.pairFinset_with_sources_eq_image_subFinset_with_source` (when `symmDiff sources_n A = B`, pairFinset_with_sources n A B = (subFinset_with_source n A).image (m ↦ (m, n - m))) + `Current.sum_pairFinset_with_sources_eq_sum_subFinset_with_source` (sum reindexing). Source-conditioned pair-weight identity (PR #880, GJ命題-unit): 主定理 `Current.sum_pairFinset_with_sources_weight_mul_weight` (under `symmDiff sources_n A = B`, `∑ pairFinset_with_sources, weight n_1 * weight n_2 = weight n * ∑ subFinset_with_source, jointFactor m (n - m)`) + 副系 `_of_mismatch` (sum = 0 when `symmDiff A B ≠ sources n`); the source-conditioned analog of PR #876. Source-conditioned Finset zero boundary cases (PR #881, GJ命題-unit, 4 lemmas): `Current.subFinset_with_source_zero` (= {0}) + `Current.subFinset_with_source_zero_of_nonempty` (= ∅ for A ≠ ∅) + `Current.pairFinset_with_sources_zero` (= {(0,0)}) + `Current.pairFinset_with_sources_zero_of_nonempty` (= ∅ if A ≠ ∅ or B ≠ ∅). Source-conditioned weight bound (PR #882, GJ命題-unit): 主定理 `Current.sum_subFinset_with_source_jointFactor_le_pow_two` (`∑ subFinset_with_source, jointFactor m (n - m) ≤ 2^(∑ e, n e)`, filter ≤ unrestricted via PR #875) + 副系 `Current.sum_pairFinset_with_sources_weight_mul_weight_le` (under `0 ≤ β J` + `symmDiff sources_n A = B`, pair-weight ≤ `weight n * 2^(∑ e, n e)`). Source-conditioned pair swap symmetry (PR #883, GJ命題-unit, 3 lemmas): `Current.pairFinset_with_sources_image_swap_eq` ((pairFinset_with_sources n A B).image Prod.swap = pairFinset_with_sources n B A) + `Current.sum_pairFinset_with_sources_image_swap_eq` (sum invariance) + `Current.pairFinset_with_sources_card_eq_swap` (card symmetric in A, B). Current.support algebra completion (PR #884, GJ命題-unit, 3 lemmas): `Current.mem_support_iff` (e ∈ support ↔ n e ≠ 0) + `Current.support_sub_subset` ((n - m).support ⊆ n.support) + `Current.support_eq_empty_iff` (support = ∅ ↔ n = 0). Current adjacency from support (PR #885, GJ命題-unit, 4 lemmas): `Current.Adj n u v` (vertices distinct + edge in support contains both) + `Current.Adj_irrefl` + `Current.Adj_symm` + `Current.Adj_of_zero_iff` (= False); foundation for switching lemma connectivity arguments. Current.toSimpleGraph realization (PR #886, GJ命題-unit, 3 lemmas): `Current.toSimpleGraph n : SimpleGraph ↑Λ` (Adj-relation-driven SimpleGraph) + `Current.toSimpleGraph_adj_iff` (= n.Adj) + `Current.toSimpleGraph_zero` (= ⊥); enables mathlib connectivity / path / component APIs. toSimpleGraph subgraph relation (PR #887): `Current.toSimpleGraph_le_inducedGraph` (Current's multigraph is a subgraph of inducedGraph G Λ, via `SimpleGraph.adj_iff_exists_edge` + support edge → edgeSet membership). The original-plan `Current.summable_weight` (Pi-type Fubini for tsum, → `Current.weightSum = ⨆ N, CurrentBounded.weightSum`) is deferred — monotone-convergence route via uniform bound suffices for the unconditional capstone. **Simon-Lieb prep — connectivity + peeling (PRs #888-#898, all under Issue #780)**: `Current.sources_card_even` (|∂n| ∈ 2ℤ, PR #894 step 98) + `Current.instDecidableAdj` + `mem_toSimpleGraph_support_of_mem_sources` (PR #895 step 99) + `Current.sum_parity_closed_eq_zero` (local handshake in closed Finset) + `sources_reachable_of_sources_eq_pair` (∂n = {i,j} → i,j connected in toSimpleGraph, PR #896 step 100) + `Current.weight_pred_edge` (exact peeling `w(n) = β*J/n_e * w(n-1_e)`) + `weight_le_mul_pred_edge` (bound `w(n) ≤ β*J * w(n-1_e)`, PR #897 step 101) + `Current.sources_sub_edge_symmDiff` (bridge: (n-1_e).sources = symmDiff n.sources endpoints(e)) + `Current.weightSum_pair_le_edge_sum` (edge-peeling bound: weightSum {i,j} ≤ β*J * ∑_{e ∋ i} weightSum (symmDiff {i,j} endpoints(e)); full proof via Finset.map+Finset.sum_map+Summable.sum_le_tsum, PR #898 step 102). Switching Lemma (PR #899 step 103): `Current.subFinset_with_source_card_switching` (|subFinset_with_source n A| = |subFinset_with_source n B| when ∂n=A△B, bijection m↦n-m via `Finset.card_nbij'`) + `Current.sum_subFinset_with_source_weight_mul_weight_switching` (∑ w(m)*w(n-m) invariant under m↦n-m, via `Finset.sum_nbij'`); the Aizenman switching lemma (GJ §5.1 Theorem 5.1.2 / FV Theorem 9.35). Simon-Lieb inequality (PR #900, step 104, GJ命題-unit, Inequalities/SimonLieb.lean):  (weightSum = ⨆N weightSum_N, RandomCurrent.lean) +  (bridge identity) +  (boltzmannWeight at h=0 = ∏_e exp(βJ spinEdgeProduct σ e)) +  (correlation A = weightSum A / weightSum ∅) +  (⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ βJ⋅∑_{e∋i} ⟨σ^{{i,j}△e}⟩; GJ §5.1 / FV Prop 9.31 p.428). **The Simon-Lieb inequality is now formally proved.** High-temperature susceptibility bound (PR #901, step 105, GJ命題-unit, Inequalities/SimonLieb.lean): `correlation_sum_le_of_high_temp` — for `0 ≤ βJ`, max degree `D`, `βJD < 1`: `∑_{j≠i} ⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ βJD/(1-βJD)` (GJ §5.1 / FV §3.7.3). Proof: iterate Simon-Lieb + fixed-point argument (T_k ≤ βJD(1+M) for all k; M ≤ βJD/(1-βJD)). Helpers: `symmDiff_pair_pair_of_ne` ({i,j}△{i,u}={u,j}) + `correlation_inducedGraph_nonneg` (≥ 0 via weightSum ratio). Infinite-volume susceptibility bound (PR #902, step 106, GJ命題-unit, Inequalities/HighTemp.lean): `susceptibilityΛ_le_of_high_temp` + `susceptibilityAlongExhaustion_le_of_high_temp` + `susceptibilityInfinite_le_of_high_temp` + `susceptibilityInfinite_latticeGraph_le_of_high_temp` — χ_∞(i) ≤ βJD/(1-βJD) at h=0, βJD<1; for ℤ^d, D=2d. Private helper `edgeFilter_card_eq_degree` connects filter.card to `G.degree v` via incidenceFinset bijection. High-temperature cluster property (PR #903, steps 107-108, GJ命題-unit, Inequalities/HighTemp.lean): `truncated2Infinite_summable_of_high_temp` (Ferromagnetic `⟨J,0,β⟩` + βJD<1 → j↦U₂(i,j) summable, via `summable_of_sum_le` + partial-sum bound through `le_of_tendsto` + `tendsto_finset_sum` + `sum_correlationAlongExhaustion_le_susceptibilityAlongExhaustion`); `clusterProperty_of_high_temp` (cluster property via `clusterProperty_of_summable`); `clusterProperty_latticeGraph_of_high_temp` (ℤ^d, D=2d). **High-temperature cluster property is now formally proved.** (PRs #850-#903, all under Issue #780.) |

The hypothesis `¬G.Adj i j` excludes the self-correlation issue: the finite-vol Simon-Lieb edge {i,j} (when i~j) gives symmDiff = ∅ → correlation ∅ = 1, but `{j,j} = {j}` as a Finset so `correlationInfinite p {j,j} = magnetization = 0` at h=0. Proof: `ciSup_le` reduces to per-stage bounds; subset case applies `correlation_inducedGraph_simon_lieb`; the edge filter sum maps injectively into `G.neighborFinset i` via `Sym2.Mem.other` (injectivity from `Sym2.other_spec`); each term bounded by `correlationAlongExhaustion_le_correlationInfinite`; sum bound via `Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg`. Three private helpers: `symmDiff_pair_pair_of_ne'` (local copy), `correlationInfinite_nonneg_of_hβJ` (nonnegativity from `hβJ` only). ℤ^d wrapper: direct application of the abstract theorem. Key private inductive lemma `correlationInfinite_latticeGraph_le_of_dist_ge`: for `dist(i,j) ≥ n+1`, `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩_∞ ≤ (βJD)^n · (βJD/(1-βJD))`. Base case n=0: per-stage one-term ≤ sum comparison (nonneg truncated2 via random-current representation) + susceptibility bound (Step 106). Inductive step: `dist ≥ n+2 → ¬Adj → Simon-Lieb (Step 109) + triangle inequality + IH on each neighbor`. Main theorem: set n = dist-1, convert `(βJD)^dist` to `exp(-(-log βJD) * dist)` via `Real.exp_log` / `Real.log_pow`; edge case βJD=0 handled separately (Lean's `Real.log 0 = 0` gives trivial rate 0; physically, βJD=0 means J=0 or β=0 so the bound holds trivially). Auxiliary additions to `Inequalities/HighTemp.lean`: `susceptibilityAlongExhaustion_latticeGraph_le_of_high_temp` (per-stage ℤ^d susceptibility bound) + `correlationInfinite_nonneg_of_hβJ` made public. Both via GKS-II β/J-monotonicity of correlation + truncated2 = correlation at h=0. (PRs #793-#795, #917, #919.) **Exhaustion-independence (Step 150, PR #984)**: `latticeMass_indep_exhaustion`: for ferromagnetic `p`, `latticeMass d Λ p = latticeMass d Λ' p` for any two exhaustions `Λ, Λ'`. Proof: `truncated2Infinite_indep_exhaustion` → set equality → sSup equality. Corollary: `latticeMass_indep_cubicExhaustion`. **Explicit lower bound (Step 152, PR #986; moved to `LatticeMassHighTemperature.lean` in PR #1926)**: `latticeMass_ge_neg_log_of_high_temp` — for d ≥ 1, 0 < βJ, βJ·2d < 1: `ofReal(-log(βJ·2d)) ≤ latticeMass`. Makes the implicit lower bound from `latticeMass_pos_of_high_temp` (Step 111) explicit as a quantitative bound. **Two-sided bound (Step 153, PR #987; moved to `LatticeMassHighTemperature.lean` in PR #1926)**: `latticeMass_two_sided_bound` — bundles Steps 115+152: `ofReal(-log(βJ·2d)) ≤ latticeMass ≤ ofReal(-log(tanh(βJ)))` for d ≥ 1, J > 0, β > 0, βJ·2d < 1. **J-lower bound on two-point function (Step 113; moved to `LatticeMassHighTemperature.lean` in PR #1926)**: `twoPointFunction_ge_tanh_betaJ_of_adj`: for adjacent `r` in `latticeGraph d`, ferromagnetic `J ≥ 0`, `β > 0`, `h = 0`: `tanh(βJ) ≤ twoPointFunction d ⟨J,0,β⟩ r`. Proof: single-edge subgraph G_single ≤ latticeGraph d; correlationInfinite G_single = tanh(βJ) by 2-site computation; GKS-II subgraph monotonicity. **Path lower bound (Step 114, PR #921; moved to `LatticeMassHighTemperature.lean` in PR #1926)**: `twoPointFunction_ge_tanh_betaJ_pow_dist`: for `r ≠ 0`, ferromagnetic `J ≥ 0`, `β > 0`, `h = 0`: `tanh(βJ)^(latticeDistance d 0 r) ≤ twoPointFunction d ⟨J,0,β⟩ r`. Proof: strong induction on n = latticeDistance d 0 r; `exists_latticeDistance_succ_adj` gives geodesic predecessor v; Step 113 handles adjacent case; for n ≥ 1 use GKS-II product inequality (`correlationInfinite_latticeGraph_cubicExhaustion_gks_second`) via {0,v} ∆ {v,r} = {0,r} with translation invariance of correlationInfinite. **Upper bound on lattice mass (Step 115, PR #923; moved to `LatticeMassHighTemperature.lean` in PR #1926)**: `latticeMass_le_neg_log_tanh_betaJ`: for `d ≥ 1`, `J > 0`, `β > 0`, `h = 0`: `latticeMass d (cubicExhaustion d) ⟨J,0,β⟩ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (-log(tanh(βJ)))`. Combined with Step 111 lower bound, gives two-sided bound `-log(βJD) ≤ latticeMass ≤ -log(tanh(βJ))`. Proof: contrapositive — if `HasExponentialDecay` at rate `α > -log(tanh(βJ))`, take axis point `r_n = (n₀,0,...,0)` with `dist(0, r_n) = n₀` (`latticeDistance_coord_eq`); Step 114 gives `tanh(βJ)^n₀ ≤ twoPointFunction d p r_n ≤ C·exp(-α·n₀)`, so `exp(ε·n₀) ≤ C` where `ε = log(tanh(βJ))+α > 0`; Archimedean gives n₀ with `ε·n₀ > C`, contradicting `exp(ε·n₀) ≥ ε·n₀+1 > C`. |
| §17.4 | Existence of Particles and a Bound on dm²/dσ | **Out of scope** | Thm 17.4.1 (particles exist: Z→0) and Thm 17.4.2 (dm²/dσ ≤ Z(σ)). Proof uses the Källén-Lehmann spectral representation S(p) = Z/(p²+m²) + spectral integral, the inverse propagator r(p) = -S(p)⁻¹, and the field strength renormalization constant Z. These are specific to the φ⁴ QFT framework (canonical single-phase model) and have no direct lattice Ising analog in this codebase. |

(Step 227, PR #1062.) **Step 228 (locally uniform on Ioo 0 J_c)**: `correlationAlongExhaustion_tendstoLocallyUniformlyOn_J_of_high_temp_open`: for `0 < β`, `1 ≤ d`: corr_n → corr_∞ locally uniformly on `Ioo 0 (1/(β·2d))` in J. Direct J-direction analogue of Step 174. Proof: Mathlib Dini locally-uniform variant + Step 227 + monotonicity in n + pointwise convergence. (Step 228, PR #1063.) **Step 229 (ContinuousAt every J ∈ Ioo 0 J_c)**: `correlationInfinite_continuousAt_J_of_high_temp`: for any `J₀ ∈ Ioo 0 (1/(β·2d))`: corr_∞ is `ContinuousAt` at J₀ (full neighborhood). Direct J-direction analogue of Step 175. Open set is a neighborhood of every interior point ⇒ Step 227 ContinuousOn restricts to ContinuousAt. (Step 229, PR #1064; Steps 222–229 now live in `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/LatticeMassHighTempLipschitz.lean`, moved from `Inequalities.lean` in PR #1929.) **Step 230 (linear bound at J = 0; right-continuity at 0)**: `correlationInfinite_le_const_mul_J_of_high_temp` (private helper `inducedLatticeGraph_correlation_le_const_mul_J`): for `0 < β`, `0 < b`, `bβ·2d < 1`, `J ∈ (0, b]`, `r ≠ s`: `corr_∞(r, s, J) ≤ (β·M² + β·4d) · J` where `M = bβ·2d/(1 - bβ·2d)`. Direct J-direction analogue of Step 176. Per-stage Lipschitz on `[a, b]` + monotonicity gives `corr_n(J) ≤ corr_n(a) + C·(J-a)`; take `a → 0⁺` using continuity of corr_n at 0 with `corr_n(0) = 0` (`correlation_zero_params_vanish_of_nonempty_A`). Right-continuity at 0 follows. Strengthens Step 224 to include J = 0 via Dini's theorem on the compact `[0, b]` with J = 0 boundary handled by trivial monotonicity and pointwise convergence. | §17.5 | Exponential-decay exhaustion transfer | **Done** | `HasExponentialDecay_transfer_exhaustion` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/LatticeMassPseudoMassTransfer.lean`): under ferromagnetic parameters, any validating exponential-decay rate for one exhaustion transfers to any other exhaustion. Proof: rewrite the truncated 2-point function by `truncated2Infinite_indep_exhaustion` and reuse the same constant and rate. This is the reusable transfer step used by the latticeMass/cluster-property bridge and by later Step 117l arguments. |
| §17.5 | Uniform high-temperature exponential decay across exhaustions | **Done (high-temperature rate)** | `HasExponentialDecay_transfer_high_temp`, `latticeMass_ge_neg_log_of_high_temp_exhaustion`, and `latticeMass_pos_of_high_temp_exhaustion` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/LatticeMassPseudoMassTransfer.lean`): the Simon--Lieb high-temperature rate `-log(βJ·2d)`, originally proved for `cubicExhaustion`, transfers to every exhaustion via `truncated2Infinite_indep_exhaustion`; consequently every exhaustion has the same explicit lower bound on `latticeMass`, and every positive-dimensional exhaustion (`d ≥ 1`) has positive mass in the strict high-temperature regime. References: GJ §5.1 pp. 74--75, §17.5 pp. 304--306 and Lemma 17.5.2 pp. 311--312; FV Prop. 9.31 p. 428. |
| §17.5 | Pseudo-mass comparison with high-temperature rate | **Done (conditional comparison)** | `HasExponentialDecay_pseudoMassFromParamsAtPair_of_le_high_temp_rate`, `latticeMass_ge_pseudoMassFromParamsAtPair_of_le_high_temp_rate`, and `latticeMass_pos_of_pseudoMassFromParamsAtPair_le_high_temp_rate` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/LatticeMassPseudoMassTransfer.lean`): if the concrete pair pseudo-mass is no larger than the transferred Simon--Lieb high-temperature rate `-log(βJ·2d)`, then `HasExponentialDecay` holds at the pseudo-mass rate; hence `ENNReal.ofReal pseudoMassFromParamsAtPair ≤ latticeMass`, and a positive pseudo-mass gives `0 < latticeMass`. This isolates the remaining quantitative comparison needed for the lower-bound side of GJ §17.5 Lemma 17.5.2 (2nd ed., pp. 311--312). |
| §17.5 | Pseudo-mass comparison exhaustion transfer | **Done (conditional comparison transfer)** | `HasExponentialDecay_pseudoMassFromParamsAtPair_of_exhaustion_le_high_temp_rate`, `latticeMass_ge_pseudoMassFromParamsAtPair_of_exhaustion_le_high_temp_rate`, `latticeMass_pos_of_pseudoMassFromParamsAtPair_exhaustion_le_high_temp_rate`, and their `cubicExhaustion` specializations (`Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/LatticeMassPseudoMassTransfer.lean`): if the pseudo-mass/high-temperature-rate comparison is verified on a reference exhaustion, exhaustion-independence of `pseudoMassFromParamsAtPair` transfers it to any target exhaustion, giving the target `HasExponentialDecay`, lower-bound, and positive-mass consequences. The cubic-reference forms make the canonical `cubicExhaustion d` comparison immediately reusable for arbitrary exhaustions. The quantitative comparison itself remains the substantive input for GJ §17.5 Lemma 17.5.2 (2nd ed., pp. 311--312). |

Proof: externalFieldEnergy_eq gives `H = interaction - h·M`, then chain/quotient rule. Analogous to Step 117a-b (β-derivative) in `BetaDerivative.lean`. (PR #931 Step 118.) |
| §4.3 | ∞-vol truncated 2-point function antitone in h (Step 125) | **Done** | `truncated2Infinite_antitoneOn_h_of_ne`: `AntitoneOn (h ↦ U₂∞(i,j)(h)) (Ici 0)` for `i ≠ j`, `J ≥ 0`, `β > 0`. Proof: Step 124 at each finite stage + `le_of_tendsto_of_tendsto`. Reference: GJ §4.3 Cor. 4.3.4; FV §3.6.3. (PR #939 Step 125.) |
| §4.3 | truncated 2-point function antitone in h (Step 124) | **Done** | `truncated2_antitoneOn_h_of_ne`: `AntitoneOn (h ↦ ⟨σᵢ;σⱼ⟩_T(h)) (Ici 0)` for `i ≠ j`, `J ≥ 0`, `β > 0`. Proof: `d/dh ⟨σᵢ;σⱼ⟩_T = β·Σₖ summand_k ≤ 0` where each summand equals `truncated3(i,j,k) ≤ 0` (GHS, distinct) or `-2·corr({m})·truncated2(i,j) ≤ 0` (degenerate). Reference: GJ §4.3 Cor. 4.3.4; FV §3.6.3. (PR #938 Step 124.) |

Proof: compose `correlation_monotoneOn_beta` (Step 122) with `pseudoMass_strictAnti` (Step 117g). Higher β → larger correlation → smaller pseudo-mass (approaching 0 at β_c). | §17.5 | ∂/∂β correlations ≥ 0 + β-monotonicity (Step 122) | **Done** | `correlation_beta_deriv_nonneg`: `0 ≤ J·Σ_e(⟨σ^{AΔe}⟩ - ⟨σ^A⟩·⟨σ^e⟩)` for ferromagnetic params. `correlation_monotoneOn_beta`: `MonotoneOn (β ↦ ⟨σ^A⟩_β) (Set.Ici 0)` for `J ≥ 0`. Proof: `monotoneOn_of_hasDerivWithinAt_nonneg` + GKS-II + HasDerivAt. Reference: FV §3.7 Lemma 3.31 part 2 (p. 107); GJ §17.5 pp. 345–347. (PR #936 Step 122.) |
| §17.6 | ∂/∂h correlations ≥ 0 via GKS-II (Step 121) | **Done** | `correlation_field_deriv_nonneg`: `0 ≤ β·(⟨σ^A·M⟩ - ⟨σ^A⟩·⟨M⟩)` for ferromagnetic parameters (β > 0 from `Ferromagnetic`). Helper lemmas: `spinProduct_mul_totalMagnetization` (σ^A·M = Σ_i σ^{AΔ{i}}), `gibbsExpectation_spinProd_mul_mag` (⟨σ^A·M⟩ = Σ_i ⟨σ^{AΔ{i}}⟩), `gibbsExpectation_totalMag_eq_sum` (⟨M⟩ = Σ_i⟨σ_i⟩). Proof: GKS-II gives `⟨σ^{AΔ{i}}⟩ ≥ ⟨σ^A⟩·⟨σ_i⟩` term-by-term. Infinitesimal form underlying monotonicity of correlations in h. Reference: FV §4.2 Prop. 4.2.4 (p. 58); GJ §17.6 pp. 348–351. (PR #935 Step 121.) |
